{"forms":[{"form":"centuries","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A period of one hundred consecutive years; often specifically a numbered period with conventional start and end dates, e.g., the twentieth century, which stretches from (strictly) 1901 through 2000, or (informally) 1900 through 1999. The first century AD was from 1 to 100."],"synonyms":[{"word":"age"},{"word":"centennium"},{"word":"yearhundred"},{"word":"centenary"}]},{"glosses":["A unit in ancient Roman army, originally of one hundred army soldiers as part of a cohort, later of more varied sizes (but typically containing sixty to seventy or eighty) soldiers or other men (guards, police, firemen), commanded by a centurion."],"synonyms":[{"word":"centuria"}]},{"glosses":["A political division of ancient Rome, meeting in the Centuriate Assembly."]},{"glosses":["A hundred things of the same kind; a hundred."]},{"examples":[{"text":"He has scored 44 limited-overs centuries.","type":"example"},{"text":"They put on a century stand to help their team recover from 57–4 to 198–4.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A hundred runs scored either by a single player in one innings, or by two players in a partnership."],"topics":["ball-games","cricket","games","hobbies","lifestyle","sports"]},{"examples":[{"text":"That was his tenth professional century.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A score of one hundred points."],"topics":["ball-games","games","hobbies","lifestyle","snooker","sports"]},{"glosses":["A race a hundred units (as meters, kilometres, miles) in length."],"topics":["hobbies","lifestyle","sports"]},{"glosses":["A banknote in the denomination of one hundred dollars."],"tags":["US","informal"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈsɛn.t͡ʃʊɹ.i/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈsɛnt͡ʃ.ɹi/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈsɛn.(t)ʃ(ə.)ɹi/","tags":["Canada","General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˈsen.(t)ʃ(ə)ɹi/","tags":["Australia","New-Zealand"]}],"upstream_line":5176,"upstream_offset":31603894,"upstream_record_sha256":"de5921aea7a72d02b5276aede2a4319e008415f59cb6450029ffc93eb8dce58b","word":"century"}
